公司簡介 
台達電為全球最大交換式電源供應器廠商 ，
為因應全球產業競爭加劇 ， 已逐步從關鍵零
組件製造商邁入整體節能解決方案提供者 ，
3Q26 營收占比：電源及零組件 (Power 
Electronics) 54% 、 基 礎 設 施
(Infrastructure) 33% 、 自 動 化
(Automation) 8%、交通部門  (Mobility) 
5%。近年 PC 產業已漸趨飽和，台達電積
極切入資料中心系統解決方案、工廠自動
化 、 樓宇自動化以及電動車及儲能應用 ， 由
傳統電源製造轉型至節能相關領域 ， 並藉由
自動化及被動元件布局提升生產效率 ， 有效
帶動獲利攀升。

主要客戶： 
主要競爭對手 ：光寶科及國外電源供應器廠
商與電源相關零組件供應商

張智皓 chihhao.chang@sinopac.com 
本次報告：法說會

台達電 (2308 TT) 
成長趨勢未變

永豐觀點 
3Q26 受到資料中心相關產品物料狀況不順影響，獲利不及預期，然預估4Q26 出
貨將轉順，獲利將回歸成長。 
 投資評價與建議 
維持買進：雖近期受到料況影響獲利狀況，然台達電受惠 AI 資料中心建置需求未
變，電源及散熱零組件規格不斷提升，台達電獲利將受惠於規格持續提升，營運能
見度佳，訂單持續累積。另一方面，台達電在電力佈局持續，SOFC 解決方案將於
2027 年開始出貨。評價方面，台達電為電源及液冷產品龍頭廠商市占率高，受惠
程度明顯，營運將進入成長期，故以2027 年稅後 EPS 56.88 元計算，目前本益比
約 26 倍，處於歷史評價區間下緣，考量台達電逐漸從過往的零組件供應商轉型成
為系統整合商的角色，有利於在產品市占及獲利能力的提升，且獲利成長性顯著，
故維持買進建議，目標價 2130 元(30X 2027-2028 平均 EPS)。

ESG 評析 
台達電企業永續評鑑整體為 A 等，於 SASB 產業永續議題與跨產業五大永續面向
平均得分分別為 B+等與 A+等。 
SinoPac+ ESG 評鑑系統評等

資料來源：SinoPac+ ESG 評等 (依循 2024 SinoPac+企業永續評鑑方法學)

專業意見，對於評分結果及評估報告內容之完整性及真實性，不負擔保責任，亦不對閱讀或使用
本評估報告之第三方負任何責任。 
註 2：永豐投顧已獲 SASB 授權，於研究中使用 SASB 重大性地圖之一般議題分類及揭露主題。

2

台達電為全球 Power 龍頭廠商：台達電為全球最大交換式電源供應器廠商，為因
應全球產業競爭加劇，已逐步從關鍵零組件製造商邁入整體節能解決方案提供者，
3Q26 營 收 占 比 ： 電 源 及 零 組 件(Power Electronics)54% 、 基 礎 設 施
(Infrastructure)33%、 自動化(Automation)8%、交通部門(Mobility)5%。 近年PC
產業已漸趨飽和，台達電積極切入資料中心系統解決方案、工廠自動化、樓宇自動
化以及電動車及儲能應用， 由傳統電源製造轉型至節能相關領域， 並藉由自動化及
被動元件布局提升生產效率，有效帶動獲利攀升。主要競爭對手為光寶科及國外電
源供應器廠商與電源相關零組件供應商。 
資料中心相關業務仍為成長主軸 ：電源及零組件部門(Power Electronics)部門營收
1059.3 億(+10% QoQ，+40% YoY)，成長主要來自資料中心電源及液冷出貨，營
業利益 199.2 億(-1% QoQ，+22% YoY)，營利率 18.8%；交通(Mobility) 部門營
收 90.7 億(+10% QoQ，+7% YoY)，受電動車需求持續疲弱，營業損失 1.37 億；
自動化(Automation)部門營收 159 億(+2% QoQ，+19% YoY)，因物料轉順，營
業利益 7.69 億元 ， 營利率4.8%； 基礎設施(Infrastructure)部門營收 658.8 億(+5% 
QoQ，+25% YoY)，成長來自資料中心液冷產品持續出貨，營業利益 120.68 億，
營利率 18.3%。整體來說，台達電主要動能來自於高階伺服器電源及散熱相關產
品，在自動化略有復甦，交通部門虧損收斂。 
供應鏈料況及資料中心建置遞延影響，3Q26 獲利不及預期：台達電 3Q26 營收
1970.59 億元(+7.5%QoQ，+31.1%YoY)，因長短料影響毛利率約 1.7%，毛利率
34.1%(-1.5ptsQoQ，-0.8ptsYoY)， 不及預期的36%， 營利率16.6%(-0.1ptsQoQ，
+0.1ptsYoY)，稅後淨利 234.95 億元(-6.5%QoQ，+26.3%YoY)，稅後 EPS 9.05
元，低於預期的 9.98 元。 
4Q26 電源及散熱產品出貨暢旺：受惠於 AI 伺服器需求持續持續增加，先前遞延
訂單將出貨，研究部上修預估 4Q26 營收 2162.3 億元(+9.7%QoQ，+33.8%YoY)，
在價格調漲及部分庫存迴轉，毛利率35.5%(+1.4pts QoQ，+0.9ptsYoY)，營利率
18.5%(+1.9pts QoQ ，+2.2ptsYoY)，稅後淨利 296.47 億元 (+26.2%QoQ，
+71.1%YoY)， 稅後EPS 為 11.41 元 。 預估2026 年營收 7588.99 億元(+36.2%YoY)，
毛利率 35.5%(+1.2ptsYoY)，營利率 17.4%(+2.3ptsYoY)，稅後淨利 988.33 億元
(+64.4%YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 38.05 元。 
AI 零組件產品內容價值提升及規模效應下，台達電成長快速：展望未來，電源產
品方面，隨著伺服器對電源需求增加，Vera Rubin 單顆 PSU 18.3kW 產品已經出
貨。此外，正負 400V DC 電源產品已小量出貨，預估將於 2H27 量產，成為未來

營運現況與分析

### Page 3

3

主要成長動能之一 。 電源ACDC 電源模組產品潛在市場持續擴大將繼續成長 ，DC-
DC 產品需求明顯回溫。散熱方面，液對氣維持高市佔率，且整體市場潛在產值仍
持續擴大，營利率亦高於公司平均，獲利貢獻顯著。整體而言，台達電在 AI 伺服
器規格提升下，成為主要受益的廠商之一，且因台達電具備關鍵零組件自製的優
勢 ， 能滿足客戶需求及控制成本 ， 長期競爭力佳 。研究部預估 2027 年營收 1.03 兆
元(+36.4% YoY)，毛利率 36.4%(+0.9pts YoY)，在規模經濟效應持續擴大下，營
利率 19.5%(+2.1pts YoY)，稅後淨利 1477.5 億元(+49.5% YoY)，稅後 EPS 為
56.88 元。預估2028 年在 800VDC 電源櫃產品及散熱產品持續放量，且燃料電池
及儲能系統將陸續貢獻營收，預估台達電2028 年營收 1.4 兆元(+36.7% YoY)，毛
利率 37.2%(+0.8pts YoY)，稅後 EPS 為 85.21 元。

### Page 1

Poya  (5904.TWO)
Strong 3Q as Expected; Tougher Comparisons Ahead
CITI'S TAKE 
Poya’s 3Q26 net profit +26% y/y came largely in-line with our forecast, and 
5% ahead of BBG consensus. SSSG of 7.0%, higher GPM, opex savings and 
stronger op leverage lifted OPM to 17.5%, the key drivers to strong 
profitability. Looking into 4Q, tougher comp should moderate sales and 
earnings growth despite fast store additions and solid operation execution. 
We maintain Neutral as the strong share price rally YTD appears to reflect 
most positives from robust SSSG and margin expansion. A higher comp 
base and potentially intensifying competition limit the room for further re-
rating. 
3Q26 broadly in-line; solid margins boosted by strong op leverage — Poya 
reported 3Q26 net profits of NT$1.0bn +26% y/y, largely in-line with our est and 5% 
above BBG’s est. 3Q sales of NT$7.4bn +15% y/y were supported by solid SSSG 
+7.0% y/y with ticket size +4.9% and traffic +2.0%. Higher GPM, strong opex 
savings and op leverage lifted OPM to 17.5%, topping our/BBG est of 17.1%/16.6%. 
T otal store count reached 495 at end-3Q26 (+25 net increase in 9M26). 
4Q outlook: faster store expansion meets a higher comp base — We expect 30 net 
store additions in 4Q26E vs. 35 stores prior, bringing the year-end store count to 
525 and supporting continued sales growth. Demand for beauty-related products 
remains healthy. However, the elevated comparison base from 4Q26E onward 
should moderate SSSG and overall sales and earnings growth, despite the 
contribution from new stores. We believe this potential growth deceleration could 
temper investor enthusiasm and trigger profit-taking pressure.
Implications: Slightly positive reaction expected; maintain Neutral — We keep our 
earnings estimates for 26-28E largely unchanged in view of the in-line 3Q results. 
We expect a slightly positive market reaction to the results given the healthy margin 
expansion. However, strong sales had likely raised expectations for 3Q profitability, 
limiting the scope for a more meaningful positive surprise. Our Neutral thesis 
remains unchanged – we believe the strong share price rally has priced in much of 
the benefit from robust SSSG and margin improvement. Looking ahead, tougher 
comp could constrain growth, while potentially intensifying competition, including 
Cosmed’s introduction of Olive Young products, could challenge Poya’s beauty-led 
momentum. We therefore see limited room for further re-rating despite solid 
operating execution. 
n
Angela Hsu AC

Bull/Bear: Poya  (5904.TWO)

### Page 5

Poya  (5904.TWO)
27 October 2026 Citi Research
5
Poya
Company description
Poya is the largest retailer of fast-moving consumer goods in Taiwan, focusing
on low-priced cosmetics, accessories, clothing and food products for females
aged 15-49. Poya had 470 stores in Taiwan at the end of 2025 and believes
that the market can accommodate up to 700-800 stores before reaching
saturation.
Investment strategy
We have a Neutral rating on Poya. We think YTD rally has baked in most

positives, including strong SSSG and solid margin expansion. While
fundamentals remain solid, tougher comparisons from 4Q26E could
moderate this year's 6-7% SSSG, lowering operating leverage and capping
further earnings upside. Meanwhile, Cosmed's expanding rollout of Olive
Young brands is intensifying competition for beauty spending. Although
Poya's SSSG has remained resilient, braoder distribution of these brands
could divert customer traffic and increase promotional pressure. We continue

to recognize Poya's execution in store expansion and ongoing efficiency
improvements. However, with these strengths largely reflected in the share
price and growth momentum facing a higher hurdle from 4Q26E onward, we
see a more balanced risk/reward.
Valuation
We derive our 12-month target price of NT$70 for Poya from a DCF valuation
method. Our assumptions include a terminal growth rate of 4.5% and WACC
of 10.1% (beta of 1.05; risk-free rate of 4%; risk premium of 7.0%; cost of equity

of 11.3%). Our target price implies a P/E of 18x on our NTM EPS.
Risks
Key downside risks that could mean the shares fail to achieve our target price
include: intense price competition among online retailers, slower store traffic,
margin dilution due to aggressive store expansion, and a soft macro
environment.
Key upside risks that could mean the shares fail to achieve our target price
include: easing price competition among online retailers, stronger store

traffic, margin expansion thanks to strong cost control, and a stronger macro
environment.

Guide to Citi Research Fundamental Research Investment Ratings:
Citi Research stock recommendations include an investment rating and an optional risk rating to highlight high risk stocks.

Risk rating takes into account both price volatility and fundamental criteria. Stocks will either have no risk rating or a High risk
rating assigned.
Investment Ratings: Citi Research investment ratings are Buy, Neutral and Sell. Our ratings are a function of analyst
expectations of expected total return ("ETR") and risk. ETR is the sum of the forecast price appreciation (or depreciation) plus
the dividend yield for a stock within the next 12 months.  The target price is based on a 12 month time horizon. The Investment

rating definitions are: Buy (1) ETR of 15% or more or 25% or more for High risk stocks; and Sell (3) for negative ETR. Any covered
stock not assigned a Buy or a Sell is a Neutral (2). For stocks rated Neutral (2), if an analyst believes that there are insufficient
valuation drivers and/or investment catalysts to derive a positive or negative investment view, they may elect with the approval
of Citi Research management not to assign a target price and, thus, not derive an ETR. Citi Research may suspend its rating

and target price and assign “Rating Suspended” status for regulatory and/or internal policy reasons. Citi Research may also
suspend its rating and target price and assign "Under Review" status for other exceptional circumstances (e.g. lack of
information critical to the analyst's thesis, trading suspension) affecting the company and/or trading in the company's
securities. In both such situations, the rating and target price will show as “—“ and “-“ respectively in the rating history price

chart. Prior to 11 April 2022 Citi Research assigned “Under Review” status to both situations and prior to 11 Nov 2020 only in
exceptional circumstances. As soon as practically possible, the analyst will publish a note re-establishing a rating and
investment thesis. Investment ratings are determined by the ranges described above at the time of initiation of coverage, a
change in investment and/or risk rating, or a change in target price (subject to limited management discretion). At times, the

expected total returns may fall outside of these ranges because of market price movements and/or other short-term volatility
or trading patterns. Such interim deviations will be permitted but will become subject to review by Research Management.
Your decision to buy or sell a security should be based upon your personal investment objectives and should be made only
after evaluating the stock's expected performance and risk.
Catalyst Watch/Short Term Views (“STV”) Ratings Disclosure:

Catalyst Watch and STV Upside/Downside calls: Citi Research may also include a Catalyst Watch or STV Upside or Downside
call to indicate the analyst expects the share price to rise (fall) in absolute terms over a specified period of 30 or 90 days in
reaction to one or more specific near-term catalysts or events impacting the company or the market. A Catalyst Watch will be
published when Analyst confidence is high that an impact to share price will occur; it will be a STV when confidence level is

moderate. A Catalyst Watch or STV Upside/Downside call will automatically expire at the end of the specified 30/90 day
period. The Catalyst Watch will also be automatically removed if share price performance (calculated at market close) exceeds

### Page 1

M
Idea
3Q GM likely to decline on higher costs and slower new product adoption…
Lotes reported unaudited 3Q26 revenue of NT$9,924M (+4% q/q, +18% y/y). Higher
copper, gold and plastics costs for legacy products, coupled with slower new
product adoption, should continue to pressure GM. We forecast 3Q GM of 44.6% (-
90bps q/q), below consensus of 45.8% (+30bps q/q). However, a decline in opex to
NT$2.0B, consistent with management commentary, should support operating

profit growth of 17% q/q to NT$2,452M, albeit still 6% below consensus. We
forecast NP of NT$2,174M (+10% q/q) and EPS of NT$19.36, 4% below the Street.
…but we see 3Q as the GM trough, with recovery beginning in 4Q: Following
slower  server platform transitions, we expect adoption to accelerate from 4Q26.
Socket production for AMD's Venice CPU will ramp from 4Q26 and socket
production for Intel’s Oak Stream CPU will ramp in 1Q27. These transitions should

enable Lotes to reprice products and pass through higher input costs, supporting
margin recovery. Our industry checks also point to a rebound in ODM server board
production in 4Q, following a modest 3Q decline. We forecast 4Q revenue of ~NT
$10.5B (+5% q/q), GM of 45.9% (+130bps q/q) and EPS of NT$21.11 (+9% q/q).
2027 - margin recovery and new AI product ramps should drive earnings growth:
Rising adoption of new CPU platforms should support socket margin recovery,

alongside growing contributions from QD, SOCAMM and NPO. QD reached 2–3% of
revenue in September, with further growth expected in 4Q26 and 2027, and we
forecast SOCAMM will reach ~7% of revenue in CY27. Lotes has two NPO
opportunities, one through LINTES and another directly, with the direct program
expected to ramp in 2H27. Although initial contributions should remain modest, we
see NPO as a longer-term AI data center growth opportunity and a potential re-

rating catalyst. Overall, we see the AI revenue mix, including QD, SOCAMM and
NPO, rising from ~5% in CY25 to slightly above 20% by CY28.
Upgrade to OW and raise PT by 15% to NT$2,350, driven by our estimate increases
for CY27-28. Our new PT implies 22x 2027e P/E and 16.5x 2028e P/E, above the past
3-year average of 17.4x, which we view as reasonable amid a rising AI revenue mix.

3Q26 Results Preview
3Q26 preview:  We forecast revenue of NT$9.92B (+4% q/q, +18% y/y), broadly in line
with the Street. Although gross margin should decline 80bps q/q to 44.6%, a 12%
sequential reduction in operating expenses should lift operating margin 270bps q/q to
24.7%. Nevertheless, this remains 130bps below consensus, implying operating profit of
NT$2.45bn, 6% below the Street. We therefore see potential for an earnings miss, as we

estimate net profit of ~NT$2,174M (+10% q/q, -6% y/y), for EPS of NT$19.36, 4% below
the Street.

Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~15%, to NT$2,350 from NT$2,045, driven mainly by our
higher earnings estimates for 2027-28.
Our new PT implies ~22x 2027e P/E, slightly above its three-year average forward P/E of
17.4x, but still below that of peer Amphenol (25-35x 2027e P/E, based on Visible Alpha
consensus). We think Lotes stock could  re-rate to a higher valuation multiple if margins
hold up well and Lotes' exposure to AI PCs and AI servers grows in the coming years.

We continue to derive our PT, which is our base case scenario value, from a residual
income model (RIM), consistent with the methodology we apply to the rest of our tech
hardware coverage universe. To calculate the intrinsic value of the stock, we use the
company's beginning equity plus the present value of all expected residual income of
earnings in excess of the cost of capital. The RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of
capital and negative when it is below the cost of capital.

Our key RIM assumptions (all unchanged) include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of ~1.0,
equity risk premium of ~8.7% and risk-free rate of ~1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our bull and bear case values rise to NT$3,400 and NT1,285, respectively, in line with
the increase in magnitude of our base case scenario.

STOCK RATINGS
Morgan Stanley uses a relative rating system using terms such as Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated or Underweight (see definitions below). Morgan Stanley does not assign ratings of Buy,
Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

months of the year), (2) new Kunshan capacity (total 1.2mn sheets/month or 21% of the
company’s current capacity) ramp up from December, and (3) the mass volume
shipment for several new AI projects (Vera Rubin + TPU v8 etc.) with a better demand
volume on the most high end M8.5/M9 CCL, we now expect EMC’s revenue to go up by
20% QoQ, and expect the GM will further expand to 40.9% (first time surpassing

For TUC, with an even faster product mix improvement speed (due to the lower base vs.

EMC) and the solid pricing hike uptrend for both high end and mid end CCL (15-20%
QoQ average pricing hike in 4Q), we expect the company to deliver a revenue growth
rate at 18% QoQ, and believe the GM will reach 40.8% (+3.9ppt QoQ) in 4Q26,
despite the slowing demand from key AI ASIC customer due to the transition period of
the ASIC projects whose capacity will be replaced by other high-end Ethernet switch
projects (also M8.5/M9 grade CCL).
Key update - EMC now plan to expand capacity to 15.25mn sheets/month from

5.85mn sheets/month capacity today, with solid demand
EMC’s BOD has approved additional capacity expansion plans extending into
2029/30E. EMC now targets year-end capacity of 8.70/10.45/11.95/15.25mn
sheets/month in 2027/28/29/30E (implying 27% 2026-30E CAGR), which is the first
time EMC announced its new capacity expansion plan after 1H28. EMC mentioned that
capacity expansion is supported by LTAs with customers who secure volumes (EMC does
not proactively initiate LTA negotiations). EMC indicated that despite its proactive

capacity expansion planned for 2027-30E, it would still be insufficient to meet with
customer demand secured under signed LTAs (demand under LTA extends at least 3Y
ahead).
EMC’s near-term capacity expansion remains intact, as the capacity expansion in
Kunshan (with additional 1.2mn sheets/month, 21% of current capacity) could
come online as early as Dec 2026 and continue through May 2027 (with new capacity
coming online every month), the expansion was originally scheduled in 2 phases, with

600k sheets/month each coming online in 1Q27 and 3Q27. The accelerated ramp-up
will offset the rescheduling of Guanyin capacity expansion, where the 300k sheet/month
expansions originally scheduled for 4Q26 have been deferred to 2H27, mainly due to
shortages of construction capacity in Taiwan as well as long equipment lead time +
Taiwan government’s strict policy on labor safety related requirement (however, we only
see delay in Taiwan, while all other plans are basically in-line or earlier than the

company’s original expectation).
Investment view - Solid margin beat + expanding margin, suggesting a stock
performance outlook; buy in the dip for both EMC & TUC
We notice that the BBG consensus for 2027 EMC/TUC GM is only at 38%/34%, and we
see upside risk to consensus EPS estimates for both EMC/TUC as the GM in 4Q could
both surpass 40-42%, without any possibility to see the GM to decrease in coming
quarters as the high-end CCL supply demand gap to further expand in coming years (see

here). As a result, we believe consensus 2027 GM could lift from 34-38% to 40-42%+ for
both companies in coming days, suggesting 10-25% EPS upward revision opportunity,
and recommend taking advantage to buy in this valuation dip (EMC/TUC only trades at
15x/12x of our bullish 2027 EPS estimate, which was far below the past 3 year range of

Moreover, EMC has mentioned all of its new capacity in coming years (capacity will

2027/28, with more room to adjust if the supply shortage issue continues.  
For the new CCL generation, we exect EMC to start shipment on M10 grade CCL from 
2H27, which is only for single customers’ AI GPU projects, but still implies a CCL 
migration trend in-line with our bullish view. For M9, we only see HGX R200 UBB and 
VR200 LPU adopt this solution in 2026, but see multiple new projects in 2027 will adopt 
this high ASP project (including several 1.6T ethernet switch projects, and Trainium 4/ 
TPU v9 could also see at least 50% adoption rate of the M9 CCL; moreover, the VR300 
rack should have 50%+ of its CCL content related to M9 grade CCL). We believe this new 
technology migration trend going into 2027 will further drive both companies’ ASP and 
profit in coming quarters, suggesting an even more favorable outlook for EMC/TUC’s EPS 
growth in coming years.  
Overall, we maintain our bullish view on high-end CCL players’ business outlook and 
margin expansion trend, and expect investors to start focusing on this solid margin 
expansion trend after this clean beat on GM/OPM in 3Q26; maintain Buy on 
EMC/TUC, with a new TP of NT$11,700/NT$3,460 (from NT$10,200/NT$3,010).

3-5+ years. We are positive on its market share trajectory in the server industry and 
expect EMC to increase its share from <10% in the Purley generation to 15-20%/20+%

leadership position. Considering its leading position in the high-end CCL industry, solid 
production skills and earnings growth outlook, we are Buy rated on the stock. With the 
shares trading at a 2026E P/E below AI server component suppliers and supported by a 
solid growth outlook, we view valuation as attractive. 
Key downside risks include: (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) 
rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and 
(3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Elite Material  
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$11,700 is based on 27x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(in line with the peak P/E multiple for AI component suppliers in the past 3 years).  
Key downside risks:  (1) RCC to replace all high-end smartphone HDI design; (2) rising 
trade tensions, which could lead to weaker smartphone and server shipments; and (3) 
rising competition from mainland China peers. 
Investment Thesis - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
TUC is a key high-end global CCL supplier that focuses on M7+ grade high-speed CCL 
(key applications include high-end switches (100G+) and AI server materials), with 20%+ 
market share in the past two years. The company continues to expect its unit market 
share in the server industry to increase from 15% in Whitley and Purley processors to 
higher in the Eagle Stream generation, and plans to launch two types of products for 
low-end and high-end customers (T2A and T2C) to gain market share. Also, strong 
growth in 400G/800G switch shipments and new 800G switch shipments, scheduled for 
launch in 2025-27, should continue to be a key demand driver for TUC, in our view. 
Additionally, the company is working on high-end AI projects with CSP and enterprise 
players, which we believe should drive revenue momentum in the long term. We view the 
stock as undervalued vs. Taiwan CCL peers on a P/E basis and rate it Buy.  
Key downside risks include: (1) slower-than-expected share gains in the low-loss CCL 
segment; (2) rising trade tensions, which could lead to weaker server and switch 
shipments globally; and (3) rising competition from mainland China peers.

Price Target Risks and Methodology - Taiwan Union Technology Corp. 
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$3,460 is based on a 22x 2H27-1H28E P/E 
(+2x STDV higher than the past 3-year industry average P/E multiple).

Greater China Technology Hardware
Asia Pacific
Industry View In-Line
In this report, we focus on China's manufacturing PMI, which we
see as a potential catalyst for AirTAC's and Hiwin's share prices.
China's manufacturing PMI fell to 49.3 in October from 50.1 in September:

• This came in lower than both our estimate and consensus of 49.9.
• The production index decreased 1.9ppt while the new order index declined
1.1ppt MoM.
Our view:
• We believe the soft manufacturing PMI data could weigh on market
sentiment in the near term. Nonetheless, we prefer supply players with
company-specific drivers such as Airtac and Hiwin:
° Airtac (1590.TW, OW) should continue to benefit from its own share
gains and incremental contribution from linear guides. Valuation appears

undemanding at 16x 2027e P/E, vs. the 25x average since 2020 (see our
30 Oct 2026 report).
° Hiwin (2049.TW, OW) should show more margin expansion in coming
quarters, supported by higher utilization and price hikes. We see
potential share price upside at the current 28x 2027e P/E, vs. peak cycle
valuation of 35-40x.
Morgan Stanley does and seeks to do business with
companies covered in Morgan Stanley Research. As a result,
investors should be aware that the firm may have a conflict of

communications with a subject company, public appearances
and trading securities held by a research analyst account.

Valuation Methodology and Risks
AirTAC International (1590.TW)
Base case, 29x 2027e P/E. We believe this methodology better captures earnings growth
momentum for industrial automation component plays (market sentiment moves along
with business cycles). As the industry enters an up-cycle, we think applying a near-peak cycle
valuation of 29x P/E is reasonable given the stronger business outlook.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger macroeconomic climate in China

n Stronger-than-expected demand from industrial automation
Risks to Downside
n Sharper economic downturn in China
n Miniature linear guideway business development taking more time
n Larger-than-expected investment in new products but weaker-than-expected demand
Hiwin Technologies Corp. (2049.TW)
Base case, P/E. We think this valuation methodology better captures Hiwin's earnings growth
momentum, as its valuation tends to move with the business cycle. We apply a target multiple

of 38x to our 2027 EPS estimate. We view this multiple as justified by the 47% operating profit
CAGR we project for 2025-28.
Risks to Upside
n Stronger industrial automation demand pickup.
n Market share gains.
n Earlier-than-expected revenue contribution from humanoids.
Risks to Downside
n Weaker industrial automation demand.
n More pricing pressure.
n Market share loss.
n More broad-based impact on the global economy from geopolitical risks.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.

Document 189: 442只翻倍基“全军覆没”，基金经理在反思什么？

Source PDF: all report pdf/442只翻倍基“全军覆没”，基金经理在反思什么？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

442 只翻倍基年内收益中位数已跌⾄ 1.6% ，逾 200 只转负，峰值 189% 的财通多策略福鑫缩
⽔⾄ 12% 。基⾦经理集体反思：过度聚焦产业逻辑，忽视筹码拥挤度与流动性⻛险，个股
分散却⾏业⾼度集中。调整后分歧显现 —— 部分经理逢低加仓 AI 算⼒，另⼀些则转向
CXO 、煤炭、内需⻰头以平衡⻛险敞⼝。
摘要：
市场震荡之际，公募基⾦三季报已披露完毕。美联储重启加息、美债收益率⾼位、海外流动性
偏紧、中东战事推升油价等变量在三季报中被反复提及。它们压缩了⾼估值成⻓股的远期定价
空间，也让基⾦经理不得不重新回答⼀个问题：为了 AI （⼈⼯智能）未来的增⻓，当下⽀付多少
价格合适？
主动权益基⾦是本轮调整中回撤最深的⼀类。《财经》根据 Wind （万得）数据，按⼆级投资分类
统计，截⾄ 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇，主动权益类基⾦年内平均回报为 -12% ，最⼤回撤平均值为 -30% ， 7671
只收益为负，占 84% 。同期全市场基⾦年内平均回报为 -7% ，最⼤回撤平均值为 -18% 。

上涨时最陡的⼀批基⾦，回落也最深。年内最⼤涨幅超过 100% 的主动权益基⾦有 442 只。截⾄
10 ⽉ 30 ⽇，这批基⾦的当前年内回报中位数已回落⾄ 1.6% ，平均回撤 51% ，其中收益率最⾼的
为易⽅达供给改⾰，收益率为 96% ，也就是说年内已⽆翻倍基。超过 200 只转为负收益，占⽐近
五成。峰值收益最⾼的基⾦财通多策略福鑫已从年内收益 189% 跌⾄ 12% 。
当⻛险信号出现时，基⾦经理为何没能及时调整？经历回调之后的 AI 资产应如何定价？当前估值
回归合理区间了吗？ AI 之外，应该投什么？基⾦经理在三季报中给出了各⾃的思考。
AI 回调后的反思
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

三季度 AI 产业链持续回调，未能及时调整的基⾦经理经受了净值回撤。从基⾦三季报中可以看
到，基⾦经理们从不同维度进⾏了反思。
⾸先是⻛险识别能⼒不⾜。
⼤成基⾦郭玮羚在季报中反思道，三季度科技成⻓板块跌幅⼤，此前重仓 AI 科技硬件，在三季度
表现糟糕，主要是 AI 产业经过上半年的⾼速发展，预期较为乐观，忽视了发展过程中阶段性的潜
在⻛险。
南⽅基⾦章晖提及，基⾦未能及时降低通信和电⼦的仓位，导致跑输基准和同业。⻓安基⾦徐
⼩勇反思道： “ 我们对市场⻅顶认识不⾜，反应⾮常滞后，导致净值回撤巨⼤。 ”
部分基⾦经理对拥挤度、流动性等问题重视不够也导致了较⼤回撤。
中欧基⾦杜厚良表⽰， “ 过于看重中⻓期产业逻辑，对短期资⾦⾯冲击的评估仍有优化空间，⻛
险控制安排有待完善 ” ，⽽且港股配置⽐例偏⾼，所持港股多为中⼩市值科技硬件标的，交易流
动性较弱。市场波动加⼤放⼤了持仓的流动性折价，抬⾼了调仓冲击成本，拖累了组合表现，
也限制了应对空间。
中欧数字经济基⾦经理冯炉丹提及： “ 需要反思的是，我们在关注产业景⽓度和公司基本⾯的同

时，对于市场持仓结构、流动性和交易拥挤度由量变向质变演化的重视程度不够，未能较好地
应对三季度的较⼤幅度回撤。 ”
国泰多资产增益先锋基⾦经理刘健琦认为： “ 机构对科技持仓的集中度升⾄历史⾼位，拥挤度本
⾝构成脆弱性，后续需更加重视拥挤度信号，做好⻛险预算管理。 ” 易⽅达彭珂亦将本轮 AI 板块回
调的触发因素归为 “ 极致拥挤的筹码结构叠加局部估值泡沫化特征突出 ” 。
⼀部分反思指向了个⼈投资框架的缺陷。
富荣福银基⾦经理郭梁良反思道，原来的框架中，景⽓反转和兑现度下降是组合减仓的依据，
但三季度的⾏情中，⾏情影响因素更多作⽤于估值，⽽⾮盈利，这导致组合对此类⻛险应对不
够敏感。
华商基⾦⾼兵也在季报中再次反思道， “ 以前的产业趋势研究框架短周期内可能失效，⽆法更好
地应对这个市场。 ”
华泰紫⾦价值甄选基⾦经理查晓磊、袁作栋、华泰紫⾦丰睿基⾦经理吴晖的追问则回到收益归
属： “ 我们也在反思，哪些是市场的 Beta （市场系统性收益），⻛格的 Beta ，哪些是真实的 Alpha

（超额收益）。 ”
⼀些基⾦经理虽然进⾏了减仓，但低估了回调幅度。
“ 我们按照事先设定的条件降低了相关仓位，但减仓主要发⽣在下跌过程中，没有在⻛险释放之
前完成，这是本季度最需要反思的地⽅。 ” 国泰基⾦赵⼤震在三季报中写道， “ 复盘以后，我们进
⼀步认识到，持有多家公司并不意味着组合真正分散，如果这些公司的收⼊和盈利最终取决于
同⼀个客⼾群体、同⼀种资本开⽀或者同⼀个产业变量，组合本质上仍然⾼度集中。后续我们
会更加严格地控制单⼀产业和单⼀需求来源的⻛险暴露。 ”
“ 尽管我们在 7 ⽉已对部分涨幅较⼤的科技股进⾏减仓，但仍低估了市场下跌的幅度。 ” 陈聪在兴全
合熙中提及。⻓城基⾦储雯⽟总结道， “ 在⼤趋势向下时，科技板块内部的调仓⽆益于回撤的控

### Page 3

制。 ” 华泰柏瑞赵楠亦坦⾔，更偏向个股的基本⾯情况，未能够提前识别市场的⻛险，果断采取有
⼒的措施控制净值的⼤幅回撤。
AI 还值得坚守吗？
AI 算⼒相关股价下跌之后，部分基⾦经理给出了最直接的⼀类回答：产业趋势没变，股价跌下
来，正是选择⼊场的好机会。
景顺⻓城基⾦杨锐⽂的转变颇具代表性。⼆季度，他还对部分个股估值超出传统定价体系⼼存
顾虑，三季度则更为乐观。他提到，经过三季度调整，市场结构性泡沫明显收敛，很多被错
杀、估值回归的机会正在出现，适合主动加⼤⼒度挖掘低估机会。
然⽽，杨锐⽂并不认为 AI 会回到⻬涨共跌的状态。 “ 科技板块⻬涨共跌的强⻉塔时代已经结束，
接下来是典型的结构性个股⾏情。 ” 他在季报中写道，⽐拼的核⼼聚焦到了底层研究选股能⼒。上
⼀季度，他的组合中⾮ AI ⽅向持仓虽受市场⻛格偏好影响阶段性承压，但相关标的估值处于历史
极低分位，同时具备扎实优异的内⽣成⻓能⼒，因此选择继续坚定持有。
⻓期押注半导体的银华基⾦⽅建动作更为积极。他在季报中坦⾔，市场如期回调，但后续调整

幅度远超出预期；全球 AI 产业趋势依然明确，半导体国产化基本⾯正在加速兑现。操作层⾯，他
在季度初稍微降了⼀些仓位，持仓结构基本维持；到季度中下旬，随着市场调整到 “ 基本⾯合适
位置 ” 时，⼜将仓位加到基本满仓⽔平。四季度，他仍将重仓低估值、⾼成⻓、⼤空间和强确定
性的半导体国产化标的，同时关注受益于 AI 浪潮的芯⽚设计和制造公司。
财通基⾦⾦梓才认为，产业链上游供给紧张的格局仍将维持，只要 AI 的⾼增⻓需求持续存在，上
游产品价格短期内或仍难回落。资本市场或需要⼀些时间，才能逐步消化这些环节的产品价格
较难回落的现实。因此，组合仅对持仓个股进⾏微调，整体板块配置保持相对稳定。
从业绩看，⽅建和⾦梓才均在上半年完成净值翻倍，⼆季度管理规模急速扩张，但三季度经历
了净值⼤幅回撤。⽅建管理的银华集成电路年内峰值收益 144.75% ，当前回落⾄ 21.59% ，该产
品规模（ A 份额 +C 份额）⼀季度末不⾜ 80 亿元，⼆季度末快速扩张⾄ 278.5 亿元；⾦梓才管理的
财通多策略福鑫年内峰值收益 189.37% ，居全市场⾸位，当前回落⾄ 12.5% ，其管理规模由⼀季

度末的不⾜百亿元升⾄⼆季度末的 565.48 亿元，三季度缩⽔ 128.84 亿元。
AI 之外投什么？
三季度⼀些基⾦经理选择从 AI 撤离，⼜投向了哪些板块？
反思减仓不够早的赵⼤震，其管理的国泰⾦⿅混合三季度净值下跌 20.1% ，过去六个⽉增⻓
49.52% 。从持仓来看，⼆季度重仓股⼏乎全部来⾃光模块、 PCB 等板块，三季度则转向了医药
研发服务⾏业。
对于科技之外的再平衡选择，易⽅达彭珂认为 AI 产业的发展和内需市场的复苏是市场中两个最重
要的要素，也是组合配置最核⼼考虑因素。虽然核⼼内需公司估值和市场预期也处于较低状
态，但预计复苏仍将需要漫⻓时间和更多政策⽀撑。 “ 基于中期布局的思路，我们适当布局了⼀
些内需⻰头公司，同时也布局了  CXO （医药研发⽣产外包）、银⾏、红利等公司，使得整体组合
⻛险暴露相对更为均衡。 ”
“ 科技产业的发展从来不是⼀蹴⽽就，⽽股票市场预期却往往线性外推或更为乐观，产业趋势本
⾝的进程和股票市场预期的错位，将是产业趋势后期科技股投资⾯临的主要⽭盾。 ” 彭珂分析，在

科技形成新⼀轮更为清晰的产业趋势前，整体科技⾏情⼤幅缩圈或难以避免，仔细甄别估值业

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

写评论
图⽚
绩匹配度⾼的公司将更为重要，先不败⽽后求胜，尽量规避⼤的回撤，将是未来⼀段时间科技
投资思路的核⼼。
易⽅达杨宗昌在⾏业配置上，增加了 CXO 、煤炭、有⾊⾦属及机械设备⾏业的配置，组合⾏业
配置更为分散、均衡。他对⼈⼯智能产业发展的前景 “ 仍然保持乐观 ” ，同时也在加⼤对传统⾏业
优质公司的研究，认为短期逆⻛可能反⽽带来好的价格和买⼊时机。
⼴发基⾦冯汉杰三季度调仓频率较低，仅对部分涨跌幅较⼤的个股做了适度调整，并在煤炭⾏
业内部及下游⾏业之间进⾏结构性微调，组合仓位与结构整体变化不⼤。展望下⼀阶段，他仍
计划保持审慎，等待基本⾯出现显著变化或估值进⼊理想的低估区间。
冯汉杰认为，尽管较多 AI 相关股票已明显回调，但当前价格所隐含的基本⾯假设仍然偏于积极；

传统⾏业诸多板块虽出现明显上涨，也不⾜以⽀撑整体⼤幅上⾏的判断。他将近两三个季度的
波动归因于短期因素扰动，从⻓期价值看，股价整体处于相对合理区间，尚未出现系统性低估
机会。
鹏华基⾦陈⾦伟继续坚守内需赛道。他观察到，三季度随着科技板块下跌，此前受 AI 硬件资⾦虹
吸效应压制的其他板块出现⼩幅修复。然⽽这种修复整体并⾮由狭义基本⾯驱动，更多源于市
场对 AI 的⼀致预期有所松动。严格意义上，传统板块三季度较⼆季度仍略有⾛弱。他仍然看好内
需类资产，包括部分消费、医药和中游周期，在基本⾯已现拐点的⽅向中选股。
本⽂来源：财经杂志

報價續漲

永豐觀點

 投資評價與建議 
研究部維持力成買進投資建議：力成 3Q26 獲利略優預期，展望 4Q26 營收和毛
利率皆季增，FoPLP 維持 2027 年年中以前可以出貨的預期，2027 年邏輯產品線
為主要營收動能，預估 2026 年 EPS 13.86 元，2027 年 EPS 22.62 元，維持買進
建議，目標價 340 元(15X 2027 EPS)。

力成為全球第五大封測廠， 以發展記憶體積體電路之封測業務為策略， 原以記憶體
測試業務起家 ，2000 年購入力晶後段設備後開始提供客戶封裝加測試一貫化服務 ， 
2012 年入股超豐電子(2441 TT) 後成為其大股東，並提供邏輯 IC 封裝業務。目前
持有超豐股權約 43%。主要客戶為鎧俠、SK Hynix(Solidigm)、美光、金士頓、南
亞科等。3Q26 業務比重為封裝 67%、測試 24%、SiP/Module 9%；產品比重為
DRAM 20%、Flash 28%、SiP/Module 9%、Logic 43%。AI 相關營收目前主要
來自 LPDDR、eSSD 等相關產品。 
3Q26 財報略優預期：力成 3Q26 EPS 3.55 元，略優預期的 3.48 元，毛利率優於
預期，主因產品組合轉佳、封測報價調漲，目前公司封測UTR 維持滿載，且4Q26
仍有調漲報價空間。 
4Q26 營收毛利率維持雙增：公司展望 4Q26 營收續增，三大產品線都將繼續調漲
報價，毛利率因為封測報價繼續調漲將提升，整體展望方向符合預期，獲利預估因
毛 利 率 優 於 預 期 而 上 修 ， 預 估4Q26 營 收 為275.54 億元 (+8.8%QoQ ，
+28.7%YoY)，毛利率 25.7% ，營業利益率 19.6% ， 稅 後 淨 利35.56 億元
(+36.2%QoQ，+90.8%YoY)，稅後 EPS 4.81 元。

### Page 3

3

FoPLP 進度維持，2027 年邏輯產品線為主要動能：公司主要營收來自 DRAM 和
NAND 封測，並耕耘高階 FCBGA 封裝、FoPLP 等技術已久，2027 年將逐步開花
結果 ， 目前公司在大尺寸晶片封裝已有拿到相關訂單 ， 客戶涵蓋AI 應用晶片業者 、
CSP 等，FoPLP 進度維持 1Q27~2Q27 放量，同樣屬邏輯產品線。公司對 2027
年記憶體市場仍持樂觀態度，表示韓廠產能沒開這麼快， 中國業者的產能則優先供
需中國本土需求仍不夠用，故供需狀況仍看不到反轉跡象。整體研究部認為 2027
年公司動能以邏輯產品為主，記憶體成長幅度相對溫和，預估 2026 年營收為
973.19 億元(+29.9%YoY)，毛利率 23.0%，營業利益率 16.6%，稅後淨利 102.31
億元 (+84.8%YoY)，稅後 EPS 13.86 元， 2027 年 營 收 為1,222.37 億元
(+25.6%YoY)，毛利率 26.3%，營業利益率 20.3%，稅後淨利 167.11 億元
(+63.3%YoY)， 稅後EPS 22.62 元 ， 維持買進建議 ， 目標價340 元(15X 2027 EPS)。

### Page 1

매수 (유지)  
좋은 실적이 상수인 구간 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다. 이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 
이전 전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 26 년 3분기 
영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 상회한 
수준이다. 
실적이 끌고, 수급이 밀고 
지금까지는 높은 실적에 대한 불안보다는 먼 미래에 대한 불확실성을 앞서 주가에 
반영시키는 힘이 더 강하게 작용하다가, 주가가 1,190,000원을 찍은 이후 상승세를 
보이고 있다. 실적을 발표한 10월 28일도 10% 넘게 상승했다. 기대 이상의 실적과 
미국 반도체 시장 분위기가 좋았던 영향이다. 앞으로는 악재에 대해서 민감도가 떨
어지는 구간에 진입할 것으로 전망한다. 지나치게 하락한 주가와 밸류에이션이 버팀
목이 될 것이고, Anthropic의 IPO까지 발표되는 Big Tech 기업들의 실적은 시장의 
우려를 충분히 상쇄할 수 있을 것으로 기대한다. 추가로 진행되는 자사주 매수도 불
안한 수급을 해소할 수 있을 전망이다. 
목표주가는 괴리율을 고려해서 하향 조정 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. 26년에도 AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 
시장은 성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 
때문이다. 26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 
판단한다. 목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 
업황에 대한 우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 27년 EPS 
538,187원에 PER 6배를 적용하였다.

 SK하이닉스 (000660)

### Page 2

SK하이닉스 (000660)

2 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 3분기, 모든 악재를 잠재운 실적 
SK하이닉스의 26년 3분기 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 31.9% 증가한 104조 
6,150억원이다.  이전 전망 대비 증가한 수준이다. DRAM, NAND 관련 데이터는 이전 
전망 대비 물량은 유사했지만 가격 상승폭은 예상보다 높았다. 
DRAM 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 35.4% 증가했다. B/G 는 +11.5%, ASP 는 
+28.2%로 추정했다. 3분기 DRAM 가격 상승률은 경쟁사 대비 높은 수준이다. Server 
DRAM 비중이 2분기 대비 높아진 것과 고객 믹스가 개선된 영향으로 추정한다. 
HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정한다. HBM이 3분기 가격에 미친 영향은 
제한적인 수준으로 판단한다.  
NAND 매출액은 26년 2분기 대비 18.2% 증가했다. B/G 는 -1%, ASP는 +26%로 
추정한다. 이전 전망치를 상회했다. SSD 비중은 2분기 75%에서 3분기에는 77%로 
상승했다.  
26년 3분기 영업이익은 26년 2분기 대비 37.6% 증가한 83.3조원이다. 이전 전망을 
상회한 수준이다. DRAM 영업이익은 지난 분기 대비 42.3% 증가한 65.1조원으로 
예상한다. 영업이익률은 전 분기 대비 4.0%p 상승한 83.0%로 추정한다. NAND 
영업이익은 전 분기 대비 23.1% 증가한 18.2조원으로 추정한다. 영업이익률은 전 
분기 대비 2.9%p 높아진 72.4%로 추정한다.

4 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
26년 4분기, DRAM 물량 효과 
SK하이닉스의 26년 4분기 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 29.4% 증가한 135.3조원으로 
예상한다. 가격상승은 지속될 것으로 예상하지만 26년 3분기 대비 상승폭이 낮아질 
전망이다. 물량은 3분기 대비 25% 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 신규 라인 가동 영향이다. 
4분기 영업이익은 26년 3분기 대비 34.1% 증가한 111.7조원으로 예상한다. DRAM 
90.9조원, NAND 20.7조원이다.  
DRAM 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 36.4% 증가한 107조원으로 예상한다. ASP 는 
+15%, Bit Growth는 +25.0%로 가정하였다. HBM 출하는 지난 분기 대비 증가할 
것으로 예상하고, HBM4 비중은 60%대로 높아질 전망이다. 
26년 4분기 NAND 매출액은 26년 3분기 대비 11.6% 증가할 것으로 예상한다. Bit 
Growth는 +5%로 전망하고, ASP는 +12%로 예상한다. SSD 비중은 소폭 개선될 
전망이다. SSD 내에서 eSSD 비중은 90% 수준이다.

IBKS RESEARCH │5 
공정 전환 속도는 예상보다 빠르게 진행 중: 1cb nm, 321L 
SK하이닉스는 1c nm 비중을 빠르게 확대하고 있다. Wafer 투입이 제한된 상황에서 
공급 확대를 위한 최선의 방법이기 때문이다. HBM 수요는 1b nm를 중심으로 대응할 
예정이다. 이와 관련된 물량도 꾸준히 증가하지만, 출하 Bit 기준으로 1c nm 비중이 
26년 1분기 3% 수준에서 26년 3분기에는 20%, 연말에는 33%까지 높아질 전망이다. 
HBM이 아닌 DRAM 물량이 크게 증가할 전망이다. 이를 통해서 높아진 DRAM 수요 
대응할 것으로 예상한다. 26년 출하 Bit Growth는 28.5%로 상향되었다. 
NAND 생산라인의 공정 전환은 High-End로 꾸준히 변화 중이다. 321L 는 26년 
2분기에는 176L와 Cross over 되었다. 예상보다 1분기 빠른 속도다. 26년 3분기 321L 
비중은 50%이다. 26년말까지 321L를 64%까지 확대할 계획이다.

6 │ IBKS RESEARCH 
Check Point 
1. DRAM ASP의 차별화 
26년 3분기 DRAM ASP는 메이커별로 차별화가 크다. 26년 2분기 대비 상승폭은 마
이크론이 10% 후반, 삼성전자는 20% 초반으로 추정하고, SK하이닉스는 20% 후반이
다. LTA, HBM 등 다양한 변수가 있지만 SK하이닉스는 고객 비중 변화가 가장 큰 변
수라고 설명한다.  
2. HBM4 물량, 수율 문제 
26년 3분기에 가장 시끄러웠던 변수가 SK하이닉스의 HBM4 관련 품질 문제이다. 26
년 3분기 DRAM 매출과 영업이익으로는 문제가 있었다고 판단하기 어렵다. 26년 3분
기 HBM에서 HBM4 비중은 30%를 상회한 것으로 추정되고 4분기 말에는 비중이 60%
까지 높아질 것으로 전망한다.   
3. HBM 8Hi 확대가 시장 규모 감소는 아니다. 
HBM4는 12Hi가 원래 제품 사양이다. HBM 공급이 부족해지면서 GPU를 좀 더 많이 
팔기 위해서 적용되는 HBM 용량을 줄이는 방향으로 제품 설계가 변경되는 중이다. 
2027년 하반기에는 8Hi 비중이 12Hi 비중을 상회할 것으로 예상한다. 하지만 이로 
인해서 HBM 전체 물량이 감소하는 것은 아닌 것으로 전망하고 있다.  
4. 투자와 생산은 다르다 
SK하이닉스는 용인팹을 공기를 앞당겨서 완공할 계획이다. 이로 인해서 공급량이 예
상 대비 커지는 것에 대한 우려가 있지만 건물 완공과 장비 반입에는 시차가 있고, 수
요를 고려하지 않은 장비 반입은 없을 것으로 밝혔다. 
5. 주주환원. 더 남아 있다 
공시를 통해서 26년 회계연도 기준 약 100조원의 주주환원 계획을 밝혔다. 배당을 30
조원 이상, 자사주 매입/소각을 60조원이상을 추진할 계획이다. 26년 4분기 중에 배
당은 14조원을 집행하고, 자사주는 20조원을 매입할 예정이다. 자사주 20조원 중에서 
10조원은 종업원 상여금 지급용이어서 주주환원에 포함되지 않는다.  
26년 4분기 실적 발표에서 추가적인 배당과 자사주 매입에 대한 계획을 발표할 것으
로 예상한다.

ASP 움직임은 메이커별로 
차별화

IBKS RESEARCH │7 
펀더멘탈과 센티멘탈의 줄다리기 구간 
SK하이닉스에 대한 투자의견은 매수를 유지한다. AI 서버 중심으로 메모리 시장은 
성장할 것으로 기대하고, 실적 대비 주가는 저평가 국면에 있다고 판단하기 때문이다. 
26년 급격히 하락한 주가는 업황, 펀더멘탈을 설명하기에는 과한 움직임이라 판단한다.  
목표주가는 4,000,000원에서 3,200,000원으로 하향한다. 메모리 시장의 업황에 대한 
우려가 아니라 현재 주가와의 괴리율을 고려한 수준이다. 2027년 EPS 538,187원에 PER 
6배를 적용하였다.  
아직 제대로 보급되지 않은 Agent AI가 확산되면 컴퓨팅 파워 필요성에 대한 인식도 
크게 바뀔 것으로 전망한다. 메타의 MUSE는 가입자당 8GB의 DRAM을 가정하고 
있다. Open AI, Google, Anthropic 에서도 Agent Platform이 공개된다면 필요한 
DRAM은 폭발적으로 증가할 것으로 전망한다. 
메모리 수요에 대한 규모가 너무 커지는 것이 걱정이지 누가 살 것인지에 대한 것은 
걱정할 문제가 아니라 판단한다. 기존 AI 모델의 진화, 효율적 모델의 등장, Agent AI 
모델의 확대 모두 메모리 수요를 기하급수적으로 확장시키는 변수이다. 
투자 주체들의 자금난이 수요를 저해하는 변수는 아닌 것으로 판단한다. 대체 
투자자들은 지속적으로 증가할 것으로 전망하기 때문이다.  
메모리 업체들의 투자 계획은 수요에 대응하기 위한 투자일 뿐 공급과잉을 만들 
수준은 아닐 것으로 판단한다.

Compliance Notice 
동 자료에 게재된 내용들은 외부의 압력이나 부당한 간섭없이 본인의 의견을 정확하게 반영하여 작성되었음을 확인합니다. 
동 자료는 기관투자가 또는 제3자에게 사전 제공한 사실이 없습니다. 
동 자료는 조사분석자료 작성에 참여한 외부인(계열회사 및 그 임직원등)이 없습니다. 
조사분석 담당자 및 배우자는 해당종목과 재산적 이해관계가 없습니다. 
동 자료에 언급된 종목의 지분율 1%이상 보유하고 있지 않습니다. 
당사는 상기 명시한 사항 외 고지해야 하는 특별한 이해관계가 없습니다.

RA 황현식 
hyeonsig.hwang@meritz.co.kr Long Trading 구간  
3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후: ESS ↑, BEV ↓  
LG에너지솔루션의 3Q26P 잠정실적 발표 이후 배터리 섹터는 11/3 미국 중간선거
를 전후로 Trading Long 투자의견이 유효하다. 미국 중간선거 이후 특정정당 우세와 
별개로, 미국 Ex-China ESS 공급망 프리미엄은 유효하다. 동사 역시 그간의 ESS 
사업 난제(단위당 수율, 팩 병목)는 점진적 해소될 점이다. 반면 시장의 BEV 반등 기
대감에 동의하지 않는다. EU 프랑스와 독일의 내연기관 퇴출시점 지연 움직임 및 미
국 연비규제 정책 폐지 결정으로 전통 완성차 고객사들향 BEV 배터리 출하 부진은 
여전하겠다. 미국의 BEV 정책 변화시점은 28.11월 대선으로 지연될 전망이다.

ESS 모멘텀: 한국 제3차 ESS 중앙계약시장 사업자 선정 모멘텀 
대다수 시장 참여자들은 2026년 연말까지 LG에너지솔루션의 미국 ESS 60Gwh  규
모의 잔여 수주 달성 가시성에 집중한다. 해당 ESS 사업 수주 가이던스 관건은 시기
보다 방향성이다. 단, 당사가 주목하는 연말 모멘텀은 한국 정부향 제3차 ESS 중앙
계약시장 사업자 선정으로 판단한다. 3차 규모는 1.13Gw로 1&2차 계약 규모(합산 
1.13Gw) 대비 확대되었다. 또한 언론보도상 LG에너지솔루션은 해당 수주를 위한 핵
심 4대 소재 등 전 Value-chain 국산화를 선언한 바 있다. 해당 수주 확정시, 동사
를 비롯한 해당 Value-chain에 긍정적 모멘텀이 예상된다.

투자의견 Buy, 적정주가 51만원 제시   
26년 연중 지정학 Risk, 에너지 대전환 영향 등에 전력 인프라 수요는 꾸준할 전망
이다. 향후 국내 이차전지 산업은 ‘L’ Shaped Cycle 전망, 반면 동사는 스마트카 T사
향 원통형 전지 출하량 증가, ESS 제조업 이상으로 변모하는 과정으로 산업 평균 성
장률 Outperform 가능하다. 투자의견 Buy 유지, 적정주가는 추정치 상향에 51만원
을 제시한다.

Meritz Research  2026. 10. 15

Buy (20거래일 평균종가 대비 상승 여력 기준) 
적정주가 (12개월) 510,000원

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439.pdf
Report source: Hankyung

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

2026-09-30

지주회사/통신서비스/ESG. 최관순 / ks1.choi@sks.co.kr / 3773-8812

SK스퀘어 경상배당수입 대비 주주환원율에 따른SK 스퀘어 주주환원 재원

Document 274: AI军备竞赛推高融资成本：美国科技企业融资溢价创2003年以来最高

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI军备竞赛推高融资成本：美国科技企业融资溢价创2003年以来最高.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-03
Pages: 2
Status: ok

### Page 1

美国投资级科技企业借款⼈的平均债券融资成本，相对于美国整体市场的溢价已达到 2003
年以来最⾼⽔平。分析指出，随着融资供给继续膨胀，美国科技债券利差可能进⼀步扩
⼤。美国科技企业正在尝试通过不同货币和市场分散庞⼤的资⾦需求。   巴黎银⾏预计，
2027 年欧元区投资级公司债券净供应量将增⻓ 83% 。
摘要：
AI 基础设施建设的融资需求持续膨胀，正在推美国⾼科技企业的债务融资成本。随着美国科技公
司竞相投⼊数千亿美元建设数据中⼼和算⼒基础设施，债券市场开始要求更⾼的⻛险补偿。
彭博汇编的数据显⽰，美国投资级科技企业借款⼈的平均债券融资成本，相对于美国整体市场
的溢价已达到 2003 年以来最⾼⽔平。
美国科技企业债券利差升⾄三年来⾼位，⽽整体投资级债券市场的利差仍相对平稳，科技⾏业
与其他发⾏⼈之间的融资成本差距进⼀步扩⼤。
美国科技巨头仍需要⼤量资⾦维持 AI 基础设施建设，但随着债券发⾏规模不断增加，投资者开始
要求更⾼收益率，以补偿潜在的信⽤⻛险和供给压⼒。分析指出，随着融资供给继续膨胀，美

国科技债券利差可能进⼀步扩⼤。
AI 融资成本⾼企，企业普遍尝试跨市场融资
数据中⼼、芯⽚和电⼒基础设施需要持续投⼊巨额资⾦，⾕歌、微软、 Meta 、亚⻢逊和甲⻣⽂
等⼤型科技公司正在通过债券市场为相关投资筹集资⾦。
美国科技企业正在尝试通过不同货币和市场分散庞⼤的资⾦需求。   巴黎银⾏预计， 2027 年欧元
区投资级公司债券净供应量将增⻓ 83% ，达到 1920 亿欧元（ 2140 亿美元），⾼于今年的 1050 亿
欧元。
不过，融资渠道增加并不意味着市场承接能⼒可以⽆限扩张。随着发⾏规模上升，投资者对新
增债券的⻛险补偿要求也在提⾼。
AI 融资⽅对融资价格相对不敏感，因为它们需要⼤量资⾦维持竞争优势。但当越来越多企业同时
进⼊债券市场，投资者的资⾦和⻛险承受能⼒终究存在边界，科技企业可能不得不提供更⾼收
益率，才能顺利完成融资。
甲⻣⽂等企业信⽤⻛险受到关注
债券市场对 AI 融资⻛险的重新定价，已经体现在部分⼤型科技企业的信⽤利差和信⽤违约互换
（ CDS ）市场中。
超⼤规模云服务商债券收益率近期上升，甲⻣⽂债券利差尤其受到关注。摩根⼤通分析师指

出，甲⻣⽂债券利差约为整体投资级债券指数的 2.8 倍，创历史最⾼⽐例。
AI 相关企业的信⽤⻛险对冲成本正在上升。Alphabet 的信⽤违约互换成本过去⼀年接近翻倍，
甲⻣⽂ CDS 报价也明显⾛⾼。

图⽚
据英国⾦融时报， AI 相关 CDS 市场交易活动正在增加，相关 CDS 净名义本⾦从⼀年前不⾜ 70 亿
美元升⾄接近 300 亿美元。英伟达五年期 CDS 利差升⾄ 141.5 个基点，反映投资者对科技⾏业债
务⻛险的关注正在扩⼤。
CDS 市场的变化并不意味着相关企业即将发⽣违约，但表明投资者正在增加⻛险对冲，并对 AI
产业链企业的偿债能⼒和未来融资需求保持更⾼警惕。
AI 投资回报与债务负担成为市场焦点
科技企业融资成本上升的背后，是 AI 基础设施投资规模与未来现⾦流回报之间的不确定性。
⼤型科技公司仍在加速建设数据中⼼和扩充算⼒，但相关投资需要持续投⼊资本，⽽投资回报
的兑现周期、 AI 业务盈利能⼒以及未来资本开⽀规模仍存在不确定性。
随着债务融资规模扩⼤，投资者不仅关注企业能否继续筹集资⾦，也开始更加重视其未来现⾦

流能否覆盖新增债务负担。

1 
Topics  Loss‑making firms are on the rise in China's growth sectors

In China, the number of companies operating in the red is increasing. And the rise is particularly striking in growth 
sectors. One factor behind the increase is the intense competition among firms, which has led to a situation in which 
only the strongest thrive. Another is that many next‑generation growth companies, despite being viewed as having great 
potential, are losing money.

■ Loss‑making firms are on the rise in growth sectors 
In recent years, the number of companies in China's 
growth sectors that are operating in the red has 
increased markedly. The share of loss-making firms in 
all listed firms (below, "loss‑making‑firm ratio") rose 
sharply from 9.6% in 2019 to 26.9% in 2025. Looking 
at the industry distribution of loss‑making firms, from 
2019 to 2022, the ratio increased mainly in 
non‑manufacturing sectors such as real estate and 
accommodation and eating/drinking services, 
reflecting the property market downturn and strict 
stay‑at‑home measures implemented to curb the spread 
of COVID‑19. Later, from 2022 to 2025, the 
loss‑making-firm ratio surged in 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices; general, production, and 
commercial machinery; electrical machine ry; and 
chemicals. These four sectors alone accounted for 
around 60% of the overall increase. 
They are the principal growth sectors in Chinese 
manufacturing. This is evidenced by the fact that the 
growth rates of value added in all four sectors exceed 
the average for Chinese manufacturing. Many of the 
high‑tech manufacturing industries that the Chi nese 
government has prioritized for development are 
included in these sectors. For example, 5G base 
stations and semiconductors fall under 
information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices. And robots fall under general, 
production, and com mercial machinery. Meanwhile, 
solar cells and batteries fall under electrical machinery, 
and biopharmaceuticals and organic chemical 
materials fall under chemicals.  
Looking at recent developments in the 
manufacturing of information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices, the global 
communications standard began shifting from 4G 
(LTE) to 5G services in around 2019, and ChatGPT was released in November 2022. These developments 
have provided tailwinds for the growth of the sector. Recently, as it has become clear that ordinary 
consumers and companies can make use of AI services on a daily basis, demand for AI‑related devices and 
semiconductors, as well as d ata centers, has soared. In this environment, many firms are focusing on 
strengthening development, manufacturing, and sales, and the sector's value added has continued to 
increase, rising from 780 billion yuan in 2022 to 910 billion yuan in 2025. Yet over  the same period, the 
number of loss‑making firms increased from 113 to 198, and the loss‑making‑firm ratio jumped from 17.2% 
to 30.1%. 
■ Competition among companies means only the strongest thrive 
One reason loss‑making firms are increasing despite operating in growth sectors is the intense 
competition among companies. The fight for market share has led to overheated investment competition

The Japan Research Institute, Limited

2 
and price competition. In information/communications 
equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing, 
the rate of growth of non -current assets from 2022 to 
2025 (+35.4%) far exceeded the growth rate of value 
added (+16.9%). This indicates that supply capacity is 
expanding faster than demand. In addition, the 
consumer price index (CPI) increased by just 0.4% 
over the same period. Service prices rose 2.2%, 
reflecting wage increases, while goods prices fell 0.7% 
due to excess supply in manufacturing. 
Information/communications equipment prices 
decreased by 3.5%, larger than the drop for goods 
overall. 
As the selection of the fittest through competition 
among companies has progressed, profits have become 
concentrated at a small number of highly competitive 
firms. The share of profits earned by the top 1% of 
firms in information/communications equipment and electronic parts/devices manufacturing rose sharply 
from 24.0% in 2022 to 43.3% in 2025. Leading companies are simultaneously establishing positions as 
both "domestic champions" and "global champions."  
■ Next‑generation growth companies are viewed as having great potential even though they 
are losing money 
However, loss‑making firms cannot simply be lumped together and labeled as losers. Globally, investors 
have shown increasing interest recently in companies with high growth potential even if they are operating 
in the red, and China has a considerable number of such firms. 
In information/communications equipment and 
electronic parts/devices manufacturing, one out of 
every two loss‑making firms is a company attracting 
strong investor interest. The Shanghai Composite 
Index rose 28.5% from 3,089 points at the beginning of 
2023 to 3,969 points at the end of 2025. 20 of the 198 
loss‑making firms in 2025 were not yet listed in 2023, 
and if these are excluded, there were 178 companies 
operating in the red as of 2025. Of these 178 firms, 96 
(53.9%) saw stock‑price increases over the period 
2023-2025 that exceeded the rise in the Shanghai 
Composite Index.  
One characteristic of these 96 companies with strong 
relative stock‑price performance is their active 
investment in R&D and intangible assets. Their 
average R&D intensity (R&D investment / sales) in 
2022 was 9.6%, and their ratio of intangible assets to 
value added stood at 60.5%. Intangible assets include 
computer software, patents, copyrights, and 
relationships with customers and suppliers. In contrast, 
the 82 loss‑making firms with weak relative 
stock‑price performance had an R&D intensity of only 
6.5% and an intangible‑asset‑to‑value‑added ratio of just 38.9%. 
As the above has shown, the increase in loss‑making firms in China's growth sectors is notable, and the 
factors behind it include not only the selection of the fittest as a result of competition among companies but 
also the emergence of next‑generation growth firms that are viewed as having great potential. 
  (Shinichi Seki)

Topics  Climate change heightens the risk of growth stagnation in Asia

Climate change could elevate the risk of economic growth flatlining in the ASEAN zone and India by causing labor 
productivity growth and capital accumulation to stall. To mitigate or avert such a scenario, establishing a business 
environment and institutions that promote "adaptation investment" by both the public and private sectors is an urgent 
task.

■ Heavy‑rain and flood damage is becoming increasingly serious in Asia 
Huge downpours and floods 
resulting in human casualties have 
been a frequent occurrence in Asia in 
the past several years. Still fresh in the 
memory is the debris flow that 
occurred in August this year near the 
border between Nepal and China, 
which as of t he time of writing has 
been recognized as a major disaster 
with thousands dead or missing. And 
in November of last year, Southeast 
Asia and South Asia saw 
record‑breaking torrential rains, 
resulting in numerous casualties, 
particularly in Indonesia and Sri  
Lanka. 
One factor that heightens the risks 
of such floods and heavy rains is 
climate change. As temperatures rise, 
the amount of water vapor in the 
atmosphere increases. Therefore, 
global warming could intensify 
torrential rainfall. Although such risk 
is shared a cross Asia, the way its 
effects are felt differs by region. 
Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and 
India are heavily affected by 
monsoons (seasonal winds), with 
many areas experiencing concentrated 
rainfall during the wet season. In such 
areas, warming can furthe r intensify 
monsoon‑driven heavy rains, 
increasing the risks of floods and 
landslides.  
■ Climate change will delay the escape from the "middle‑income trap" 
Climate change not only results in human casualties by way of natural disasters, but may also undermine 
the long‑term economic growth potential of ASEAN and India through various channels. The Asian 
Development Bank (ADB) [2024] conducted a quantitative an alysis of the extent to which climate change 
will weigh on Asian economies over the long term. Based on the IPCC's "very high GHG emissions" 
scenario (SSP5‑8.5), the analysis assumes a 4.7°C rise in the Earth's average temperature by 2100 relative 
to pre-industrial levels, and estimates the difference in real GDP between that and a hypothetical scenario 
without climate change. The results illustrate the long‑term impact on economic growth through declines 
in labor productivity, stagnation in capital accumulation, and downw ard pressure on consumption due to 
sea‑level rise, floods, heat waves, and other phenomena caused by climate change.  
An examination of the results reveals that in every country/territory, the pushdown effect on real GDP 
relative to the no‑climate‑change scenario expands over time, with 2035, 2050, and 2070 each seeing 
greater impact. The pushdown effect for ASEAN and Ind ia is −15.0% to −8.8% in 2050 and −30.2% to

−18.1% in 2070, larger than the effects for China (2050: −4.8%, 2070: −10.3%) and Central Asian countries 
(2050: −1.7%, 2070: −3.8%). Within Asia, the ASEAN zone and India can be considered regions where the 
risk of economic stagnation due to climate change is relatively large.  
This may make it even more difficult for ASEAN countries and India to escape the "middle‑income trap." 
Although their income levels have risen, they still remain far from high‑income status. Indonesia, Malaysia, 
the Philippines, and Thailand became middle‑income countries in the 1990s or earlier, but have still not 
been able to join the groups of high‑income countries. Furthermore, Vietnam and India, which became 
middle‑income countries in the 2000s or later, have yet to enter a high‑growth trajectory like China once 
did. Climate change, by causing labor productivity growth to stall amid rising temperatures and companies 
to curtail investment due to more frequent disasters, may lead to income growth in the ASEAN zone and 
India being delayed further.  
■ Improving the environment to accelerate "adaptation investment" will be important 
To alleviate the pushdown effect of climate change on growth, expansion of "adaptation investment" by 
the public and private sectors is essential. Specifically, this could include improving working environments 
and promoting measures to lighten workloads a nd reduce headcounts in order to offset declines in labor 
productivity caused by rising temperatures, as well as developing infrastructure and strengthening facilities 
to prevent damage to production equipment from sea‑level rise and floods. However, ASEAN  and India 
cannot be said to have sufficiently 
developed the foundations for public 
and private promotion of such 
adaptation investment.  
As part of its Global Adaptation 
Initiative (ND‑GAIN), the University of 
Notre Dame (U.S.) evaluates countries' 
ability to use investment to achieve 
climate‑change adaptation in the form of 
a "readiness" score with three 
components: economic readiness, 
governance readiness, and social 
readiness. Respectively, these 
components assess 1) whether a 
business environment conducive to 
adaptation investment by private -sector 
companies has been put in place, 2) 
whether the effectiveness of institutions

and policies to support private-sector adaptation investment has been secured, and 3) whether tangible and 
intangible social infrastructure that promotes investment has been established. 
Looking at readiness in 2024, ASEAN countries and India generally rank in the middle to lower tiers 
globally, with a large gap between them and developed countries. Notably, the Philippines, India, and 
Thailand fall outside the top 100, indicating weak momentum for adaptation investment. Breaking down 
the scores, problems are especially evident in the economic and governance categories. India and Thailand 
have particularly low eco nomic readiness, ranking 113th and 124th, respectively, and five countries 
(Malaysia not included) fall outside the top 100 for governance readiness. Given this situation, to accelerate 
private‑sector adaptation investment, India and Thailand in particular  need to move swiftly to improve the 
financial environment to make it easier to procure necessary funds domestically and internationally, relax 
regulations to encourage the launch of new businesses, and reform labor markets to enable the smooth 
securing of human resources. In addition, for Asia as a whole, it will also be important to boost confidence 
in the institutional factors that enhance application of investment for adaptation by increasing policy 
stability, ensuring the effectiveness of legal systems, and curbing corruption.

(Tomohiro Hosoi)

Document 309: Cliff Notes_ managing expectations (2026-11-03)

#cliffnotes #rba #australia #inflation #us #fomc #ecb #aud #usd
Cliff Notes: managing expectations
By Ryan Wells, Illiana Jain & Elliot Clarke
11:04 October 23 2026 Share
Key insights from the week that was.
With little domestic data of significance, participants’ attention focused on RBA Deputy Governor
Hauser's fireside chat, which saw an abrupt shift in market expectations of a follow-up rate hike at the
November meeting.
Hauser warned in his remarks that the current inflation outlook was "materially worse" than the RBA's

### Page 2

August forecasts. Three upside inflation risks were characterised as having "crystallised" ahead of the
September meeting – the Middle East, the AI boom, and domestic supply constraints. Hauser noted the
Middle East picture "looks worse" given oil prices are "about a third higher than [the RBA] assumed in
August”. He then positioned AI and data centre developments among an "extraordinary sequence of
positive demand shocks around the world", with "higher AI prices, tech prices feeding through into

CPI". Finally, on domestic capacity pressures, the Deputy Governor noted that "inflation's been coming
in at or a little bit above expectations, despite an apparent easing in the labour market". He went on to
state that the staff are currently assessing "whether [the RBA] need to change [its] judgement on
capacity pressures in the labour market again" – in other words, revise up their assumption for the
NAIRU.
Hauser's comments prompted a material reassessment of market pricing for a follow-up November

hike, from just a 20% chance at the end of last week to a 50/50 proposition today ahead of next week's
Q3 CPI. For that release, we are forecasting a 1.3% (4.1%yr) lift in headline inflation and a 0.9 (3.5%yr)
increase in trimmed mean inflation. Our preview provides more detail behind the component mix for Q3
as well as our upward revisions to the preliminary Q4 forecast. Provided inflation prints broadly in line
with our expectations, the RBA is likely to hike by 25bp at the November meeting.

Across in New Zealand, consumer prices rose 0.7% in Q3 and annual inflation eased from 4.1%yr to
3.7%yr. Under the surface of this ‘as expected’ read though, several closely watched measures of core
inflation took a step higher to now be at or slightly above the top of the RBNZ’s target band. Our NZ
economics team consequently expect the RBNZ will raise the OCR 25bps to 3.00% at its October
policy review, while retaining a 4.00% terminal OCR forecast for September 2027.

Earlier in the week, China's economy surprised to the upside in Q3, GDP growth accelerating from
0.9%qtr to 1.2%qtr, lifting annual growth from 4.3%yr to 4.5%yr – keeping authorities on track to
achieve their 4.5-5.0% full-year growth objective.
While annual retail sales growth improved in September, rising from 0.4%yr to 1.1%yr, the current pace
of growth is only a quarter of the gain seen just a year ago and very weak versus history. Consumer
spending continues to be constrained by weak confidence, particularly with respect to employment

prospects and wealth. On housing wealth, five years into the property downturn, house prices continue
to decline month-on-month and investment remains in a deep downturn, -20.6%ytd.
Total fixed asset investment has been detracting from annual GDP growth since April owing to a lull in
high-tech investment, a lack of confidence in future demand amongst domestically oriented businesses
as well as property’s malaise. For aggregate growth to hold up through 2027 and 2028 as the

contribution from net exports moderates, broad-based stimulus in scale will prove necessary. Expect
recent targeted support measures to be broadened and given much greater scale in coming months.
Finally, in the UK, inflation accelerated from 3.1%yr to 3.5%yr in September, exceeding both market
expectations and the Bank of England's July forecast. The surprise was largely driven by energy, fuel
prices rising 5.4%mth. However, core inflation also edged up from 2.6%yr to 2.8%yr as services

inflation increased from 3.4%yr to 3.7%yr. While not an immediate concern inflation, the labour market
showing resilience, the ILO unemployment rate falling from 4.9% to 4.7% in August, suggests demand

### Page 3

Related articles
risks for inflation will need close monitoring as well as supply-side concerns.
Next week will see the focus once again return to the US as the FOMC meet and US Q3 GDP is
released. For both events, the implications for risks to the outlook will be the focus. The week after, the
US mid-term elections are due.

Economy 
Kicking and screaming: Child care costs persist 
20 October 2026

Key takeaways 
• Child care remains a growing financial burden for many households, even as growth in the median payment has moderated, 
according to Bank of America payments data. More families are paying more than $2,000 per month for care, suggesting 
affordability pressures are becoming increasingly concentrated among households facing the highest expenses. 
• Child care cost pressures vary significantly across the country. Nashville and Jacksonville, FL posted the fastest year-over-year 
growth among major metro areas, while New York and Washington DC saw the biggest decrease in median payment growth, 
according to Bank of America payments data. 
• Limited supply may continue to constrain access to child care for some households. Child care employment fell 0.7% year-over-
year in September, suggesting provider capacity remains under pressure and could continue to affect household finances and 
labor force participation.

Child care costs outpace rent and medical services 
Child care remains a significant expense for many families. In September, the growth in child care and other school-related fees 
surpassed other major household expenses such as rent and medical services, according to the Bureau of Labor Statistics (BLS) 
(Exhibit 1). 
Yet, while child care prices continue to rise in official inflation data, median household payments have largely moderated since 
February, according to Bank of America data (Exhibit 2). More importantly, the growth in the number of households paying for 
child care has fallen since the start of 2025.

changing the amount of care they use, which is reflected in slower growth in the number paying for child care. As more

The decline in the number of households paying for child care may also reflect families seeking alternatives to formal care 
arrangements. Some may be relying more heavily on relatives, shifting parents’ work schedules or dropping out of the workforce 
altogether to care for their children in response to elevated costs. This could explain why, among all income groups paying for 
care, the number of lower-income households has seen the biggest decline, falling 2.0% year-over-year (YoY) in September 
(Exhibit 3). 
Exhibit 3: The number of lower-income households paying for child care has fallen the most, down 2.0% YoY in September 
Number of households with child care payments (monthly, three-month moving average, YoY%)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
More families face larger monthly bills 
Yet even as fewer households pay for child care, the share of those shelling out more than $2,000 per month has increased 
substantially (Exhibit 4). In fact, it has more than doubled since 2023, reaching 7.7% in 2026 year-to-date. Meanwhile, the share 
of those paying $1,000 or less has declined, suggesting higher monthly payments are becoming more common even as the 
national median grows more slowly. 
Exhibit 4: The share of households paying >$2000 a month for child care has more than doubled since 2023 
Monthly child care payments by amount (annual average, share of households, %)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Rising costs take a bigger bite out of households’ paychecks 
What makes child care “affordable”? According to the U.S. Department of Health and Human Services, child care is considered 
affordable if it accounts for no more than 7% of family income. Yet, in Bank of America data, the average child care cost was 8% 
in September.  
While an 8% share may not appear dramatically above the federal affordability benchmark of 7%, it represents an average across 
households’ income levels. For families in high-cost metro areas or those paying more than $2,000 per month, the burden is 
likely considerably higher.

Where child care costs are climbing 
Child care pressures also differ substantially by location. In Bank of America payments data, Nashville posted the fastest YoY 
median payment growth in September, followed by Jacksonville, FL and Phoenix (Exhibit 6). This dispersion of growth across the 
country suggests child care cost pressures are no longer confined to historically expensive coastal markets.  
Interestingly, New York and Washington DC saw the sharpest decline in growth this year. Why might this be? The supply side of 
the market may help explain why – for example, New York City recently introduced universal Pre-K.1

Fewer providers, higher costs 
As of September 2026, employment in child care services was down 0.7% from a year earlier, suggesting the sector’s workforce 
recovery has stalled. Fewer workers can constrain the number of available places or the hours providers offer. That may leave 
families facing longer searches, fewer workable arrangements or continued price pressure. In turn, access to care can influence 
parents’ ability to enter, remain in or expand their participation in the workforce. 
Exhibit 7: Employment within child care services fell 0.7% YoY in September 
Employees by sector (monthly, seasonally adjusted, YoY%)

Source: Haver Analytics  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
What are the economic implications of rising child care costs? 
Child care costs may have important labor market implications for families with young children. Of those households paying for 
child care in 2026, more than half of them were Older Millennials (52%), followed by Younger Millennials (20%) and Gen X (16%), 
according to Bank of America payments data. This means child care costs weigh more heavily on Older Millennials. 
The share of Older Millennial households receiving more than one paycheck has continued to grow from a year earlier, but the 
pace of that improvement has moderated over the past three months (Exhibit 8). Given that this group accounts for the majority 
of households paying for child care in Bank of America payments data, they may offer early signs of how affordability affects 
their employment decisions. 
Exhibit 8: The share of Older Millennial households paying for child care that have more than one person with a paycheck has declined since 2021  
Share of households with a child care payment with more than one person with a paycheck (%, monthly, three-month moving average)

Source: Bank of America internal data  
BANK OF AMERICA INSTITUTE 
Child care challenges can weigh on women’s employment 
According to data from the American Time Use Survey, women continue to perform significantly more child care and household 
work than men, even as their participation in paid work has increased. This suggests that persistent gaps in access and 
affordability may have disproportionate consequences for women’s employment. Indeed, the percentage of women who cited 
child care as a reason for temporary unemployment was nearly three times higher than men’s in 2026, though the average fell 
from 2025, according to data from the BLS. 
Yet labor force participation data indicates a more nuanced picture. Despite persistent child care pressures, mothers are 
participating in the workforce at rates that remain above their 2019 averages.

Methodology 
Selected Bank of America transaction data is used to inform the macroeconomic views expressed in this report and should be considered in the 
context of other economic indicators and publicly available information. In certain instances, the data may provide directional and/or predictive 
value. The data used is not comprehensive; it is based on aggregated and anonymized selections of Bank of America data and may reflect a 
degree of selection bias and limitations on the data available. 
Bank of America credit/debit card spending per household includes spending from active US households only. Only consumer card holders 
making a minimum of five transactions a month are included in the dataset. Spending from corporate cards is excluded. Data regarding 
merchants who receive payments are identified and classified by the Merchant Categorization Code (MCC) defined by financial services 
companies. The data are mapped using proprietary methods from the MCCs to the North American Industry Classification System (NAICS), 
which is also used by the Census Bureau, in order to classify spending data by subsector. Spending data may also be classified by other 
proprietary methods not using MCCs.  
If applicable, the consumer deposit data based on Bank of America internal data is derived by anonymizing and aggregating data from Bank of 
America consumer deposit accounts in the US and analyzing that data at a highly aggregated level.  
If applicable, any payments data represents aggregated spend from US Retail, Preferred, Small Business and Wealth Management clients with a 
deposit account or credit card. Any reference to aggregated spend include total credit card, debit card, ACH, wires, bill pay, business/peer-to-
peer, cash and checks.  
Unless otherwise stated, data is not adjusted for seasonality, processing days or portfolio changes, and may be subject to periodic revisions.  
Additional information about the methodology used to aggregate the data is available upon request.

Contributors 
Taylor Bowley 
Economist, Bank of America Institute

### Page 1

币安史上最⼤盗窃案 ——63 亿美元资产被劫，⿊客慌不择路，反成套利者的 " 提款机 " 。急
于脱⼿稳定币的攻击者不计成本强⾏换币，在各链各平台留下价差漏洞，⼀名前量化交易
员五分钟内赚得 25% ，机构套利者彻夜在 " ⿊客交易的另⼀⾯ " 收割。⿊客越乱，市场越扭
曲，利润越丰厚。
摘要：
币安遭遇史上最⼤规模盗窃案， 63 亿美元资产被盗，却意外为⼀⼩批⾏动迅速的交易者创造了
丰厚利润。⿊客越慌乱，亏得越多，交易者赚得越快。
据彭博社周⼆报道，急于将赃款变现的⿊客不惜以⼤幅折价成交，在多条区块链和交易平台上
留下了⼤量价格错配。据区块链数据公司 Bitquery 估计，攻击者因仓促出⼿导致执⾏损耗⾼达约
1.19 亿美元。这笔钱，相当程度上流⼊了早有准备的套利者⼝袋。
币安于 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇发现安全漏洞后，随即暂停提现，这⼀举措在⽆意间加剧了市场割裂 —— 原本
⼏秒内即会消失的跨平台价格缺⼝，被迫延续数分钟乃⾄数⼩时，为交易者提供了可乘之机。
区块链情报公司 TRM Labs 的数据显⽰， Tether 和 Circle 在以太坊链上的攻击发⽣约 40 分钟后冻

结了价值约 18 亿美元的稳定币，⽽在此之前，⿊客已疯狂完成⼤量换币操作。
⿊客的逃命交易：急于脱⼿稳定币，不计价格
攻击者在控制币安资⾦后，⾸要⽬标是处置赃款中的稳定币。由于 USDT 等稳定币可被发⾏⽅冻
结，⿊客必须抢在冻结令下达前，将其兑换成⽆法被追索的资产，如以太币（ Ether ）。
在约半⼩时内，攻击者开始通过 Uniswap 等去中⼼化交易所激进换币，⽆论价格⾼低，在任何
有流动性的地⽅⼤量买⼊。这种 " 不计成本、单向猛冲 " 的操作模式，在流动性本就薄弱的去中⼼
化市场中造成了极度扭曲的价格。
D2 Finance 创始⼈兼⾸席投资官 Luca Parlamento 表⽰，他的公司整晚都在 " ⿊客交易的另⼀
⾯ " 操作。他透露，攻击者通过去中⼼化交易所购买以太币的平均成本约为 2,657 美元，⽽同期加
密 货 币 衍 ⽣ 品 平 台 Hyperliquid 上 的 报 价 仅 约 为 2,300 美 元 。 " 这 个 价 差 本 该 由 我 们 来 填
补， "Parlamento 说。

Caerus Global Management ⾸席投资官 James Aitchison 将这种市场状态描述为 " ⼀个资⾦规模
庞⼤、对价格极不敏感的单⼀卖家在薄弱的流动性中强⾏出货 " 。他表⽰，这种激进的单向抛售
在不同交易场所和区块链之间制造了多处显著错配， " 我们利⽤这⼀事件进⾏了交易，并发现了
⼏个极具吸引⼒的错配机会。 "
套利者的教科书操作：五分钟内赚 25%
对于早有资⾦部署在相关平台的交易者⽽⾔，操作逻辑简单直接 —— 在⿊客拉⾼价格的地⽅卖
出，在价格未受波及的地⽅买⼊，赚取中间差价。
前量化交易员、衍⽣品交易所 QFEX 创始⼈ Annanay Kapila ，在伦敦市中⼼的办公室⼯作时敏锐
地捕捉到了这⼀信号。他发现以太币在去中⼼化交易所上的价格急剧飙升，部分交易所报价⼀
度突破 3,000 美元。 Kapila 随即在去中⼼化借贷平台 Aave 上以稳定币为抵押借⼊⼤量以太币，转
⽽在价格⾼位的交易所悉数卖出。他表⽰，整个操作在五分钟内完成，利润达到 25% 。

Kapila 直⾔，近年来加密货币市场波动趋于平稳，此类机会越来越罕⻅，上⼀次类似窗⼝要追溯
到 2025 年 10 ⽉的市场暴跌。 " 今年加密货币市场有些沉寂。每当出现重⼤市场失衡，就会带来巨
⼤机会， " 他说， " 我从 2021 年就⼊场了，那时这种事⼏乎每周都在发⽣，但如今相对罕⻅了。 "
机器⼈的价格战：费⽤飙升，半数收益被算法截获
⿊客的慌乱不仅波及以太坊，也蔓延⾄其他链和代币。
Aitchison 透露，攻击者在 Tron 链上通过⼩型去中⼼化平台 SunSwap 分 16 笔交易，以平均约 36.7
美分的价格买⼊约 6.38 亿枚 TRX ，⽽当时市场均价约为 33 美分。这波买⼊⼀度将该交易池价格
推⾼⾄⼤盘价格的 18% 以上，价格缺⼝历经约 10 ⼩时才完全弥合。

另⼀处明显异常出现在 Ripple 旗下美元稳定币 RLUSD 上 —— 攻击者将 4,800 万美元的 RLUSD 骤
然倾倒⼊⼀个流动性极浅的买盘池中，导致其价格瞬间跌⾄ 81 美分，随后数分钟内迅速回归 1 美
元平价。
如此显著的价格错配，迅速引发交易机器⼈的激烈竞争。由于区块链⽹络按⽀付⼿续费⾼低来
决定交易处理顺序，各家算法争相抬价以求优先成交，推动⽹络费⽤急剧攀升。
据 Bitquery 统计，攻击者因执⾏不⼒损失的约 1.19 亿美元中，约⼀半被交易者和套利机器⼈收⼊
囊中；另有约 2,950 万美元以⼿续费形式流向了以太坊区块的打包⽅，后者在机器⼈竞价中坐收
渔翁之利。
不是所有⼈都下场：⼊场⻔槛决定收益上限
并⾮每家机构都选择参与这场混战。 Waves Digital Assets 国际投资组合管理负责⼈ Rajiv
Sawhney 表⽰，其团队全程监测了相关价格差异，但最终选择按兵不动。他指出，最⼤的获利
机会属于那些事先就已在正确位置部署好资⾦的机构。
Sawhney 同时提⽰了这⼀交易的局限性：窗⼝期极为短暂，交易成本在事件期间⼤幅飙升，部

分借贷市场也随着夜⾊渐深开始收紧活动限制。
币安已于周⽇开始分阶段恢复提现，并表⽰与⾏业合作伙伴协⼒冻结了约 30 亿美元的相关资
产，并动⽤⾃有资⾦补⾜被盗损失。

Document 527: スペインは解散総選挙へ

2026 年 10 月 7 日

MURC Focus

スペインは解散総選挙へ

～右派連立政権が成立する見込み～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 解散総選挙に臨んだサンチェス首相 
スペインのペドロ・サンチェス首相が 10 月 5 日、議会を解散し、11 月 29 日に総選挙（定数 350、比例代表制）
を行うと発表した。住宅価格や家賃の高騰に対する政府の緊急政令案が、10 月 2 日に下院で否決されたことを
受けて、少数与党による政権運営の行き詰まりを意識した首相が、国民に信を問う決断をした形である 。ただし、
サンチェス首相が率いる中道左派の与党・社会労働党（PSOE）の支持率は低迷している（図表 1）。

もともと PSOE の人気は低迷しており、前回 2023 年 7 月の総選挙でも、ライバル政党である中道右派の国民
党（PP）が第一党となっていた。しかしサンチェス首相は他の左派政党や地域政党の協力を受けて組閣し、政権
を維持した経緯がある。しかし首相周辺の汚職疑惑などから PSOE の人気は回復せず、次期の総選挙では PP
と同根の右派政党である Vox による連立政権が成立する公算が大きい。

（年、四半期）
〇スペインのサンチェス首相が10月5日、議会を解散し、11月29日に総選挙を行うと発表した。次期の総選挙
では野党第一党の PP と同根の右派政党 Vox による連立政権が成立する公算が大きい。 
〇PP と Vox が総選挙で勝利すれば、2018年6月以来の政権交代が実現する。こうした右派回帰を民族主義
の台頭という流れに位置付ける向きもあるが、スペインの国内事情はもっと複雑である。 
〇仮に総選挙を経て PP と Vox の連立政権が成立しても、両派間ですり合わせるべき課題も多く、スペイン政
治が安定するとは限らない。ゆえに、当面の問題への対応も進まないだろう。

### Page 2

解散の直接的な理由となった住宅価格の問題であるが、住宅市況が底にあった 2015 年を基準（＝100）とす
る指数でその動きを確認すると、直近2026 年4～6 月期は201 と、ユーロ圏全体（159）を大きく上回る（図表2）。
特に 2023 年以降の上昇が目覚ましいが、堅調な景気や移民の急増を受けて住宅需要が高まったことがその主
因である。

2. 民族主義的な右派回帰とは異なる 
PP と Vox が総選挙で勝利すれば、2018 年 6 月以来の政権交代が実現する。こうした右派回帰を民族主義
の台頭という流れに位置付ける向きもあるが、スペインの 国内事情は複雑であるため、必ずしもその流れに合わ
ない点、留意すべきである。まず、そもそも PSOE の人気そのものが低下している。確かに景気は堅調だが、住
宅価格の急騰のような社会問題に関して、サンチェス政権は具体的な処方箋を提示できなかった。

総選挙を経て政権交代が実現し、PP が Vox と連立を組んだとしても、それを民族主義の台頭の表れと理解す
るよりも、一定の期間ごとに繰り返される左派から右派へのリバランスの流れとして理解するほうが適切だろう。 ま
たスペインの場合、右派間に対立関係があることにも注目する必要がある。地域ナショナリズムが強いスペインで
は地方分権を求める保守の力が強く、中央集権を主張する保守（つまり Vox）と対立関係にある。

中央集権を望む保守の一部に戦前・戦後のフランコ体制を理想化する向きがあることも、地域ナショナリズムと
の対立を生んでいる。地域ナショナリズム政党の中には、自らを保守と位置付けながらも、中央集権を望む保守と
の対立から、PSOE や他の左派政党（Podemos や Sumar）と歩調を合わせる動きもある。スペインの民族主義は
多様なため、同国の右派回帰を反欧州連合（EU）や反移民の文脈で捉えることはできない。

例えばスペイン南東部のカタルーニャ州では、伝統的に左派も右派も民族主義を掲げ、独立運動（あるいは高
度な自治の獲得のための運動）を展開しているが、同時に票を奪い合う関係にある。最近は左派の カタルーニャ
共和主義左翼（ERC）が劣勢である。一方、右派のカタルーニャ同盟（AC）が優勢となっており、政党支持率では
PSOE に次ぐ第 2 位となっている。

AC は右派だが独立を主張しており、中央集権を主張する Vox とは対立する。一方、カタルーニャ州でも独立
に同調しない有権者は数多く、そうした有権者は PSOE に投票する傾向が強い。カタルーニャ州はスペインでも
有数の産業地帯であるため、労働運動が盛んだったことから、伝統的に PSOE の支持が厚いためである。このよ
うに、スペイン政治はまだら模様であるため、右派回帰と民族主義を同一視することは不適切である。

### Page 3

3. 総選挙後の新政権も多難な船出となる見込み 
総選挙後にいかなる立場の新政権が成立しようとも、スペイン経済の先行きに対する懸念は残る 。確かに景気
は堅調を維持しているが、一方、冒頭で述べた住宅価格の高騰のような社会問題に対する妙薬はなかなか存在
しない。加えて、経済成長の次なる牽引役を見いだせていないという問題もある。例えばスペイン景気を牽引する
輸出だが、旅行サービスに支えられたサービスの輸出は好調でもモノの輸出は不調である（図表3）。

中南米からの移民による労働供給が潤沢とはいえ、グローバルな労働力不足やスペインの人口構成（純増で
はあるが移民流入に依存した増加）に鑑みれば、スペインでも生産性の向上は不可欠である。それに資する総固
定資本形成は、実体として建設投資に偏っており、機械投資や知的財産投資の規模は限定的である（図表 4）。
これらの投資を増やすためには国による産業政策が欠かせない。

具体的には、規制緩和や税制優遇といった措置である。これまではサンチェス首相率いる PSOE 政権が少数
与党だったということや、PSOE が中道左派であるため経済成長よりも所得分配を重視したこともあり、そうした設
備投資を後押しするような政策が必ずしも打ち出されたわけではなかった。では PP と Vox の連立政権に交代し
たことでそれが可能になるかどうかというと、その可能性も高くない。

なぜならば、PP と Vox の連立政権が強いイニシアチブの下で産業政策を“上から”推し進めようとすると、中央
集権的な色彩が強くなり、地域ナショナリズムとの間であつれきが生じる展開が予想されるためである。中央集権
志向を弱めるという現実的な判断を Vox ができるかどうかが鍵を握るが、同党にはもともと PP から右派色の濃い
グループが離脱して結党した経緯があるだけに、その余地がどの程度か定かではない。

現状では総選挙を経て PSOE から PP と Vox の連立へと政権が交代する公算が大きいが、両党ですり合わせ
るべき課題も多く、政治が安定するとは限らない。ゆえに、当面の問題への対応も進まないだろう。

Document 531: バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持

Source PDF: all report pdf/バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持.pdf
Report source: MURC
Date: 2026-10-06
Pages: 4
Status: ok

### Page 1

2026 年 10 月 6 日

MURC Focus

バーナム新政権も財政拡張路線を堅持

～10 月末発表の秋季予算案で具体的な財源を示せるか～

調査部 主任研究員 土田 陽介

1. 伝統的な財政拡張を訴えるバーナム新政権 
英国では、9 月 27 日から 30 日にかけて、北西部の主要都市リバプールで与党である労働党の党大会が開催
された。7 月 20 日に就任したアンディ・バーナム首相にとって初の党大会となると同時に、新政権の財政運営方
針が示されるという点で、この党大会は非常に高い注目を浴びた。この場で示されたのは、中道左派である労働
党らしい、伝統的な財政拡張路線に基づくプランだった。

具体的には、バーナム 新政権の関係閣僚らは、政府調達（政府が民間からモノやサービスを購入すること）の
増強による景気浮揚策を主張した。例えばジョン・ヒーリー財務相は 28 日の党大会で、スコットランドのクライド海
軍基地に浮きドック 3 基を建設する他、海洋調査船を建造することに数十億ポンドを投資する計画を発表した。
いずれも国内の業者に発注することで雇用や所得につなげる方針である。

また労働党大会に先立つ 26 日、バーナム新政権は、新築住宅を初めて購入する国民向けに新たな住宅購
入支援策を導入すると発表した。保守党政権下の 2013 年から 2023 年にかけて実施された“Help to Buy”と似
たスキームであり、購入者は購入価格の 2.5%に相当する頭金を支払えば、政府が購入価格の 20%までを融資
するというものである。

このように、バーナム新政権が目指す方針は、労働党に伝統的な“大きな政府”による財政拡張路線を反映し
ている。そうした点で、バーナム新政権の方針はキア・スターマー前政権とはあまり変化がない。一方で、新政権
が示した財政拡張路線が金融市場の評価を得るためには、財源を明示する必要がある。10 月 28 日に発表する
秋季予算案で、金融市場が納得する内容を示せるかが鍵を握る。

〇9月末にリバプールで開催された労働党の党大会で、バーナム新政権の財政運営方針が示されたが 、それ
は中道左派である労働党らしい伝統的な財政拡張路線に基づくプランだった。 
〇一方、金融市場 では、それまではバーナム 人気によって労働党 の政権運営が安定するとの思惑から“英国
買い”が先行していたが、党大会前後から調整色を濃くしている。 
〇増税は有権者の反発を、国債発行が投資家の反発を生むと予想される中、秋季予算で財政拡張の財源の
筋道をきちんと示せなければ、バーナム新政権も短命で終わりかねない。

### Page 2

2. 財政拡張を続けてきた近年の英国 
バーナム新政権は財政拡張に活路を 見いだそうとしているが、これまでも英国の政権は財政拡張に臨んでき
た（図表 1）。例えば英国の実質 GDP（国内総生産）に占める公需（＝政府支出＋公的固定資本形成）は、保守
党政権（テリーザ・メイ元首相）期の 2018 年後半に 21.5%を下回るまで低下したが、以降は上昇し、直近 2026
年 4～6 月時点で 24.4%となっている（図表 1）。

この間、2020 年 1～3 月期に生じたコロナショック後の公需の減退も限定的にとどまっており、結局は公需が
GDP に占める割合は“V 字回復”することとなった。労働党が政権を奪取したのは 2024 年 7 月の総選挙だが、
それ以前の保守党政権下でも、英国は事実上、財政拡張を続けていたことになる。その結果、公的債務残高の
対 GDP 比率も、一時は高インフレのために低下したが、再び上昇した（図表 2）。

このように近年の英国は、保革の立場を問わず財政拡張を続けてきた。しかし、EU 離脱やエネルギー不安に
伴う供給制約の高まりの中でそうした手法を取り続けたため 、英国はスタグフレーション（景気停滞と物価高進の
併存）に突入した。その改善のために必要な処方箋は、規制緩和を通じた供給の刺激である。しかし、それは“小
さな政府”を追求することにもなるため、中道左派として“大きな政府”を目指す労働党は容認できない。

バーナム新首相は地方の主導の経済成長を訴え、そうした意味での分権化に意欲的と考えられる。 ただし労
働党としては、公需による民需の刺激を通じた景気浮揚を引き続き志向していることが、秋の党大会で示された
形である。一方、金融市場では、バーナム新首相就任直後は、首相の人気に支えられて新政権の運営が安定す
るとの思惑から“英国買い”が先行していたが、党大会前後から調整色を濃くしている。

### Page 3

3. 財源なき財政拡張で信用を失うリスク 
スターマー前政権もまた、財政拡張路線による景気浮揚を目指した が、同時にそれは、供給の底上げを狙うも
のでもあった。老朽インフラ（鉄道や水道など）の再開発はその最たるものである。またスターマー前政権は、財政
規律にも配慮し、財源を国債発行よりも増税でカバーし、 財政拡張策の整合性を取ろう とした。財源を示したこと
を投資家は評価したが、増税に有権者が反発したため、結局、スターマー前政権は短命に終わった。

スターマー前政権下で低迷した労働党の支持率は、バーナム人気に支えられ“V 字回復”し、右派の改革党
（UK Reform ）と肉薄するに至る（図表 3）。労働党としては打ち出した目玉政策を着実に遂行し、有権者の支持
をさらに確たるものにしたいところである。とはいえ、そうした目玉政策は財政拡張を伴うものであるため、財源をど
う確保するか明確な筋道を示さなければ、金融市場の動揺を誘う。

すでに英国の長期金利（10 年国債流通利回り）は、グローバルなインフレ加速懸念に伴う世界的な金利上昇
の波に飲まれ、上昇が続いている（図表 4）。金融市場は財政拡張に対して神経を尖らせているが、秋季予算で
明確な財源が示さなければ、それを嫌気した投資家が英国債（ギルト債）の売却を進め、金利が一段と上昇し、
場合によってポンド安と株安の“トリプル安”を引き起こす可能性もある。

財政拡張の財源を増税で賄おうとすると、スターマー前政権のように有権者の支持を失いかねない。 一方、国
債発行で賄おうとすると、長期金利の押し上げ圧力になる。ゆえに、歳出の見直しで財源を賄おうとするだろうが、
簡単ではないため、具体的な筋道を示せなければ、結局は金利の上昇につながる。秋季予算で財政拡張の財
源の筋道をきちんと示せなければ、バーナム新政権も短命で終わりかねない。
